zz_md_company_slot_matches = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = { scope:actor = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = $SOURCE$ target = flag:$BUILDING$ } } }
        has_domicile_building = $BUILDING$
    }
    trigger_else = { NOT = { has_domicile_building = $BUILDING$ } }
}

zz_md_company_attachments_match = {
    zz_md_company_slot_matches = { BUILDING = merchant_company_building_civic_01 SOURCE = $SOURCE$ }
    zz_md_company_slot_matches = { BUILDING = merchant_company_building_production_01 SOURCE = $SOURCE$ }
    zz_md_company_slot_matches = { BUILDING = merchant_company_building_trade_01 SOURCE = $SOURCE$ }
    zz_md_company_slot_matches = { BUILDING = merchant_company_building_warehouse_01 SOURCE = $SOURCE$ }
    zz_md_company_slot_matches = { BUILDING = merchant_company_building_civic_02 SOURCE = $SOURCE$ }
    zz_md_company_slot_matches = { BUILDING = merchant_company_building_production_02 SOURCE = $SOURCE$ }
